Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-3..-2, 0, 3)
find at_most_two: bool
find b: bool
find c: bool
find zero: bool
find mixed: int(-3..3)
find quotient: int(-3..3)
find remainder: int(0..1)

such that

mixed = product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]),
(zero) <-> (product([x,toInt(b);int(1..)]) = 0),
product([x,toInt(and([b,x != 0;int(1..)]));int(1..)]) = mixed,
quotient = (x / sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)])),
remainder = x % sum([toInt(b),1;int(1..)]),
(at_most_two) <-> (sum([toInt(b),toInt(c),toInt(x % 2 != 0),toInt(x != 0);int(1..4)]) <= 2)

--

mixed
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
mixed
new variables:
  find mixed#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find mixed#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find mixed#sign_magnitude_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (mixed#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (!(mixed#sign_magnitude_3)) -> (or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(mixed#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (mixed#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([mixed#sign_magnitude_1,mixed#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [mixed#sign_magnitude_1,mixed#sign_magnitude_2,mixed#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) >= -3,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [mixed#sign_magnitude_1,mixed#sign_magnitude_2,mixed#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [mixed#sign_magnitude_1,mixed#sign_magnitude_2,mixed#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) >= -3
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*mixed#sign_magnitude_1, -2*and([mixed#sign_magnitude_3,mixed#sign_magnitude_1;int(1..)]), 2*mixed#sign_magnitude_2, -4*and([mixed#sign_magnitude_3,mixed#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)])) GreaterEqual -3) using None with []

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [mixed#sign_magnitude_1,mixed#sign_magnitude_2,mixed#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) <= 3
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__1
